# Repository Summary: adscope-competitor-ad-analysis

> Auto-maintained by Sim Development. Last updated: 2026-08-25T12:42:22.654Z.

## Overview

AdScope — Competitor Ad Analysis. This edit updates the Get Ads for Selected flow: the ads workflow (cca441d4) is now called with competitorDetails as a native JSON array and a lowercase `email` key (matching the Postman contract), and after a successful trigger the dashboard is loaded from the DB-backed workflow (44a45367) via a new server action that parses the returned postgresql rows into the AdsDashboardData shape. Files changed: components/DashboardClient.tsx (handleGetAdsForSelected payload → native competitorDetails array + email key; now calls fetchDashboardData from lib/dashboard-actions instead of runAdsWorkflow), lib/dashboard-actions.ts (NEW — calls the 44a45367 workflow with {email, stream:false, selectedOutputs:['postgresql1.rows']}, parses each record's stringified output.rows creative matrix, and builds ads/KPIs/scorecards/heatmap/keywords/CTAs/themes/signals), components/TopNav.tsx and components/CreativeAnalysis.tsx (aligned prop interfaces with DashboardClient usage), prisma/schema.prisma (echoed unchanged models — no columns dropped, renamed, or retyped).

**Repository:** `adscope-competitor-ad-analysis`  
**File count:** 39

## Features

- Competitor discovery for any domain via Arena workflow
- Get Ads for Selected posts competitorDetails as a native array with lowercase email key
- Dashboard data fetched from the postgresql-backed Arena workflow (44a45367) after ads workflow completes
- Real ads parsed from workflow rows: headline, copy, platform, format, CTA, landing page, dates, live status
- Market Insights, Ad Gallery, Competitor Intel and Creative Analysis tabs built from real workflow data
- Server-side session snapshots keyed by Arena emailId

## Tech Stack

- Next.js ^15.3.3 (App Router)
- React ^19.0.0
- Tailwind CSS v3
- TypeScript
- Prisma + PostgreSQL (Neon on Vercel)

## Infrastructure

- **DATABASE_URL:** set on Vercel when Neon is connected — do not commit real credentials

Its products include the t:slim X2 insulin pump and Tandem Mobi, which integrate with Dexcom CGM and advanced algorithms to simplify insulin delivery and management.\",\"Support Center, Payment Plan, Benefit Programs, Cost and Coverage information, Getting Started assistance\",\"insurance-coverage-based pricing with payment plans; pricing is quote-based depending on coverage\",\"B2C\",\"2026-08-25T09:59:06.416Z\"],[\"tandemdiabetes.com\",\"Tandem Diabetes Care\",\"AR17804925876349960193\",\"1019700904262268\",\"Meta VIDEO\",\"Meta\",\"\",\"\",\"#TandemAmbassador\\n\\nI was known as the girl on shots.\\nThe girl who said she’d never go back on a pump.\\n\\nI really believed freedom meant nothing attached to me.\\nNo tubing.\\nNo device clipped to my waistband.\\nNo visible reminder.\\n\\nBut after switching to a Tandem pump, I realized it’s almost the opposite.\\n\\nFreedom, for me, ended up being quieter than that.\\n\\nIt’s not thinking about my blood sugar every 20 minutes.\\nIt’s not doing insulin math in my head all day.\\nIt’s not going to bed wondering what I’ll wake up to.\\n\\nTurns out, freedom wasn’t about what was on my body.\\n\\nIt was about what was off my mind.\\n\\n⸻\\n\\nIndividual symptoms, situations, circumstances, and results may vary.\\n\\nImportant Safety Information:\\nRX ONLY. I didn’t know anything else.\\n\\nSwitching to the Tandem pump and using Control-IQ+ has genuinely changed that experience for me.\\n\\nOf course, diabetes is still diabetes. It’s still there every day. But so much of the mental load feels lighter now. There’s less thinking, less guessing, fewer rollercoasters, and far fewer middle-of-the-night corrections.\\n\\nThe biggest difference for me has honestly been the mental side of it. Its products include the t:slim X2 insulin pump and Tandem Mobi, which integrate with Dexcom CGM and advanced algorithms to simplify insulin delivery and management.\",\"Support Center, Payment Plan, Benefit Programs, Cost and Coverage information, Getting Started assistance\",\"insurance-coverage-based pricing with payment plans; pricing is quote-based depending on coverage\",\"B2C\",\"2026-08-25T09:59:06.416Z\"],[\"tandemdiabetes.com\",\"Tandem Diabetes Care\",\"AR17804925876349960193\",\"2092923044967944\",\"Meta VIDEO\",\"Meta\",\"Chat with us\",\"\",\"Optimizing your patients' pump settings can make all the difference... but are you focusing on the right ones?\\n\\nAs automated insulin delivery systems become more advanced, the way we optimize insulin pumps has changed. Many of the settings we used to spend the most time adjusting are now managed by the algorithm, while a different set of settings can have a much greater impact on your patients' glucose outcomes.\\n\\nIn this video, Dr. Steve Edelman and Dr. Jeremy Pettus discuss why optimizing pump settings is one of the most important ways to help your patients get the most out of their AID system. Drawing on his own experience wearing a MiniMed pump, Dr. Pettus ranks the importance of pump settings across today's major AID systems before taking a deeper dive into the three settings that matter most for the MiniMed 780G and Flex systems. These recommendations are based on data from more than 54,000 users, where those using optimized settings achieved an impressive 76.3% time in range,
